## 1. Executive Summary

- Malanta identified large-scale pre-attack infrastructure staging across 76 seed IOCs, expanding to 34,800 indicators, including 34,702 cluster-sourced IoPAs, within an evidence graph of 40,038 nodes and 140,265 edges.
- Five clusters define the risk surface; three are very large and high risk: A96E2B1E-67F... with 13,744 domains, C896C720-972... with 8,462 domains, and 36A5F609-771... with 14,293 domains.
- Classifications show 1,819 malicious and 32,579 suspicious indicators; examples of malicious-labelled domains include 65346.xyz, 67663verifycard-booking.com, 8293785.shop, adrianfrieg.com, and atomicawealth.com, all p=1.0.
- APT attribution: Low confidence / not supported by provided evidence; no actor-specific infrastructure, tooling, TTP, or campaign identifiers were provided.
- Code-repository signal: seed declaration.linkpc.net appeared in URLHaus with 61 co-occurring indicators.
- Overall confidence: High for infrastructure clustering and IoPA scale; Low for actor attribution due to insufficient evidence.
